# Champion A OOF Targeted Diagnostic

Diagnostic date: 2026-10-05 (JST). DIAGNOSTIC-ONLY; accepted immutable row-level evidence; no reconstruction.

## 1. Scope and immutable evidence

Artifact fact: initial reviewed HEAD `d51ca330d0ca50329f0b47b0add64b41ef2dbbe4`; initial working tree clean. Only accepted 2020-2024 Champion A OOF rows and their manifest were consumed. Historical raw/source match tables, operational bundles, 2025, and 2026+ outcomes were not loaded.

Authoritative rules: [freeze specification](CHAMPION_A_OOF_DIAGNOSTIC_FREEZE_SPEC.md), especially sections 10-16; reviewed pure implementation: `src/modeling/champion_a_oof_diagnostic.py` at commit `6481780f208ffe95294bdb426a3e572a82a34a98`.

Artifact fact: CSV `data/processed/model_diagnostics/champion_a_oof_2020_2024.csv`; manifest `data/processed/model_diagnostics/champion_a_oof_2020_2024_manifest.json`. These are immutable evidence, not model inputs or a new formal evaluation.

## 5. Gate A evidence

Every class/direction is evaluated. C1: overall same sign >=4/5. C2: the SAME fixed bin, n>=30, same sign >=3/5. C3: nonsparse-bin union n>=30, same sign >=4/5. C4: core n>=30 with same sign >=3/5. C5: localization share <0.80 in >=3/5 of those SAME core-supporting years. C4/C5 are conjunctive in the implementation; marginal localization counts cannot replace the joint count.

| Away | 1 | FAIL 1/5 | FAIL max=2/5 | FAIL 1/5 | FAIL 1/5 | FAIL 1/5: 2020 | FAIL |
| Draw | -1 | FAIL 1/5 | FAIL max=1/5 | FAIL 1/5 | FAIL 1/5 | FAIL 1/5: 2023 | FAIL |
| Draw | 1 | PASS 4/5 | PASS max=4/5 | PASS 4/5 | PASS 4/5 | PASS 4/5: 2020,2021,2022,2024 | PASS |
| Home | -1 | FAIL 3/5 | PASS max=4/5 | FAIL 3/5 | FAIL 2/5 | FAIL 2/5: 2020,2022 | FAIL |
| Home | 1 | FAIL 2/5 | PASS max=4/5 | FAIL 2/5 | PASS 3/5 | PASS 3/5: 2021,2023,2024 | FAIL |

Diagnostic result: Gate A = PASS. The final decision is the unchanged return value of the implementation's frozen pure `decide()` inside `diagnose()`; the evidence table is an explicit rendering, not a replacement rule.

## 6. Transition localization

Frozen narrow subgroup T = promoted_involved OR any_team_first5. Frozen core = established_only AND NOT any_team_first5. Directional mass = max(direction * (1[result=class] - p_class), 0), summed without cancellation. Share >=0.80 = TRANSITION_CONCENTRATED. Both directions for all classes are reported, including non-recurrent ones; no class/direction was selected after looking at the result.

### Frozen comparison deltas

Delta = left group minus right group, within the SAME validation year. Positive LL means the opening/first-five group has higher mean NLL. These are descriptive differences with different outcome mixes, NOT causal excess loss. Support requires BOTH n>=30 and LL delta >1e-12. Brier consistency requires delta >=-1e-12. Pooled comparisons are context only.

## 9. Gate B evidence

Gate B is a diagnostic decision candidate ONLY if Gate A fails. Its components are still reported for transparency. A = all opening/middle support; B = all any-first5/neither support; C = BOTH established-only LL comparisons support in the same year; D = BOTH established-only Brier deltas nonnegative within tolerance. The implementation requires C AND D in the SAME >=3/5 years, not separate marginal counts.

| Year | A all opening/middle | B all first-five | C established both LL | D established both Brier | C AND D |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 2020 | PASS | PASS | PASS | PASS | PASS |
| 2021 | FAIL | FAIL | FAIL | FAIL | FAIL |
| 2022 | PASS | FAIL | FAIL | FAIL | FAIL |
| 2023 | PASS | PASS | PASS | PASS | PASS |
| 2024 | FAIL | PASS | PASS | PASS | PASS |

| Component | Support / 5 | Threshold result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A | 3/5 | PASS |
| B | 3/5 | PASS |
| C | 3/5 | PASS |
| D | 3/5 | PASS |
| CD | 3/5 | PASS |

Diagnostic result: Gate B evidence = PASS; decision eligibility = NOT CONSIDERED (Gate A has priority). D's marginal count is context; it does not establish B without the joint established-only LL/Brier recurrence.

## 10. Favorite-failure context

Frozen independent flags: home_favorite p_home>p_away; away_favorite p_away>p_home; near_even abs(p_home-p_away)<0.05. Favorite and near-even cells may overlap and MUST NOT be summed as an exclusive partition. All three true classes are reported for every flag; losses/draws are explanatory context and cannot open a new gate.

## 13. Decision

### Headline diagnostic findings

- Away overall OVERPREDICTED and Draw overall UNDERPREDICTED recur in 2021/2022/2023/2024 (4/5 each). Their nonsparse-bin unions also support 4/5. Away core/localization joint support is 2021/2022/2023/2024; Draw core/localization joint support is 2020/2021/2022/2024. Both class/directions pass every Gate A component.
- Strongest fixed-bin recurrence is tied at 4/5: Away overprediction [0.3,0.4); Draw underprediction [0.2,0.3); Home overprediction [0.3,0.4) and [0.5,0.6); Home underprediction [0.2,0.3). Home's bins do not override its failed overall/nonsparse recurrence requirements.
- Away overprediction localization shares range from 0.3138089913077049 to 0.37353372913677557; Draw underprediction shares range from 0.28626596003471905 to 0.41300549332335323. All five years are NOT_CONCENTRATED for these directions, so this is not a >=0.80 narrow-transition mass concentration. Core n is 204/261/199/205/236 by year.
- Gate B all-population opening/middle support: 2020/2022/2023 (3/5); any-first5/neither support: 2020/2023/2024 (3/5). Established-only BOTH LL comparisons with BOTH nonnegative Brier deltas: 2020/2023/2024 (3/5). Gate B evidence meets its rules, but Gate A priority means no separate transition-lane decision is made.

Frozen pure decision result: `PROCEED_TO_CALIBRATION_RESEARCH_SPEC`.

Gate A = PASS; Gate B evidence = PASS. Priority is calibration, then season transition, then inconclusive. The reported component tables were checked against this unchanged implementation result.

Authorization meaning: proceed only to a separate calibration RESEARCH SPEC task. It does not choose a calibrator, authorize fitting/evaluation, or demonstrate improved Log Loss. No Platt/isotonic/temperature/beta/draw-uplift/class-weight family is selected here.

## 14. Interpretation limits

All results reuse 2020-2024 historical OOF evidence. They do NOT prove unseen generalization. Calibration bias recurrence is a diagnostic hypothesis, not proof that any calibrator improves LL. Bias magnitudes are not converted to estimated LL improvements. Season/group differences retain class-mix/confidence confounding and cannot establish causal offseason effects. SPARSE points cannot independently open a lane; NONSPARSE is a raw availability label, not statistical certainty. Five-year denominators remain fixed; pooled evidence is context. The report adds no feature recommendations beyond the gate meaning.
